# wait-for-FS gate sweep - the /results books

`ON` is the gated engine. `OFF` is the same engine with the gate
switched off, so the difference is the gate and nothing else.

**Two kinds of entry, and the rule only covers one of them:**

- **fall-through** - the entry is on a mark, not a print. This is what
  the rule is about and what the gate removes. It must be 0 on the
  gated engine.
- **own-FS** - the entry IS the swing's own FS print, the swing
  declaring itself. Safe by construction, and the owner has said so.
  It is still counted because the chain's confirmations are NOT
  monotonic: consecutive legs can share a direction, so the previous
  element of the chain can confirm AFTER this swing's own FS.
